Entry 1
Category: Communication & Computing
Invention: Printing Press
Inventor(s): Johannes Gutenberg
Year: c. 1440
Significance: Enabled mass production of books and spread of knowledge.
Key Points:
- Landmark invention that influenced science, technology or society.
- Frequently covered in general knowledge and history of science.
